, 
				ordCompanyId,
				ordCompanyPersonId,
				ordProjectId, 
				@AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				@LanguageID, 
				@RegisterDatePersian		 
			FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
			/*RZ960225 for multi language And add length variable*/
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			/*RZ960225 dar inja nesbat dehi be @DraftId anjam mishavad*/
			SELECT @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
				(
				   [DocID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[SendAddress]
				 )
			SELECT 
				@DraftId,
				OL.LanguageID,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID)
			FROM dbo.WSOrder_Lang OL 
			INNER JOIN dbo.WSOrder O ON OL.OrderId = O.OrderId
			WHERE O.OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/ 
		/*---------------------------------*/
		END
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSDoc' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------
		--===================================================================
		--===================================================================
		--===================================================================*/
		/*Declare @DraftId INT 
		Select @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()*/
		/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
		/*RZ960225 dalile jabejaie declare @DraftId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be DraftId jadid niaz darim*/
		/*chon dar zaman darj draft be @DraftId id jadid nesbat dade shode inja be 2 dastor bala niazi nist*/
		SET @InsertedDraftID = @DraftId
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*---------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL, @ordIPrdId INT = NULL, @ordIQuantity float = NULL /*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
			,@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @ordIWHId INT = NULL
			,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
			,@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			,@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
			,@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL/*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			 /*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			 ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL
			 ,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT  = NULL
			, @TmpI FLOAT = NULL
			 /*RZ14010621- End*/
			 ,@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*--*/
			DECLARE crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR /*--For OrderItems*/
				SELECT 	
					OrderItemID,
					ordIPrdId,
					ordIQuantity,
					ordIDescription,
					ordIFCR, ordIFCRI, /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
					ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
					,ordIPrdUnitId/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
					,ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
					/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
					,ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					/*RZ14010621- End*/
					,ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM dbo.WSOrderItems
				WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
				AND ordIWHId = @WareHouseId /*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
				AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
				/*RZ14020606- End*/
			/*--*/
			OPEN crsr
			FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, @ordIDescription,
									  @ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
									  @ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
									  ,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
									  ,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
									  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
									  ,@ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*--*/
			WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 
			BEGIN
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------------- WSDocItems --------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
				IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM @TmpTable))
				BEGIN
				/**/
					DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
					SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP 1 [Quantity] FROM @TmpTable WHERE OrderItemId = @OrderItemID)
					IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, @ordIDescription,
												  @ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, @ordITracing,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
												  ,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
												  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
												  ,@ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
						CONTINUE
					/**/
					END
					ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
					BEGIN
					/**/						
						/*RZ14011005- Calculate QuantityTotal*/
						IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrQty,0))
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @ordIQuantityTotal = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
						/**/
						END
						/*RZ14011005- End*/
						SET @ordIQuantity = @CurrQty
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14000628- End*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
				SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
				SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
				SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
				SET @TmpI = NULL
				/*--------------*/			
				SELECT 				
					@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
					@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
					@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductId = @ordIPrdId
				/*--------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				/*----------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0) /*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
					SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)/*RZ14010629- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14010621- End*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems (
					DocId,
					PrdId,
					Quantity,
					[Description],
					UserId,	Month1,	FPId, RegisterDate, RegisterDatePersian,
					WHId, AppType,
					/*ADBF951130*/
					LanguageID,
					FirstRegisterDatePersian,
					FirstRegisterUserId,
					FirstRegisterDate
					,[FCR], [FCRI] /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */ 
					,[Tracing]/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
					,[PrdUnitId] /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
					,[QuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
					/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
					,[PrdUnitIdI]
					,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					/*RZ14010621- End*/	
					,[PrdNickname]  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					,[OrderItemId] /*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
				) 
				VALUES(
					@DraftId,
					@ordIPrdId,
					@ordIQuantity,
					@ordIDescription,
					@UserId, @Month1, @FPId, GETDATE(), @RegisterDatePersian,
					/*@WhID,*/
					ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
					@AppType,
					/*ADBF951130*/
					@LanguageID,
					@RegisterDatePersian,
					@UserId,
					GETDATE()
					,@ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */ 
					,@ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
					,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
					,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
					/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					/*RZ14010621- End*/	
					,@ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					,@OrderItemID /*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
					)
				/*--*/
				DECLARE @DocItemId INT = NULL
				SELECT @DocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
				/*Begin insert records in lang table*/
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems_Lang (
					[DocItemID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[Description]	
				   ,[PrdNickname]  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				) 
				SELECT
					@DocItemId,
					OILN.LanguageID,
					OILN.ordIDescription,
					OILN.ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM dbo.WSOrderItems_Lang OILN 
				WHERE  OILN.OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
				/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
				/*End insert records in lang table*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------------- WSDocItemSerials --------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
				BEGIN TRAN
				/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
					/*RZ960225 for multi language */
					/*----------------- Begin user cursor for insert serial in doc -------------------*/
					/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @SerialId INT = NULL
					/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
					DECLARE @ordItemSerialDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL
					DECLARE @docItemSerialId INT = NULL
					/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
					DECLARE Crsr_Add_Serial CURSOR LOCAL For
						/*RZ14000628- Insert docItemSerials to number  (@ordIQuantity)*/
						SELECT  TOP (CAST(@ordIQuantity AS INT))/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
							SerialId,
							[Description],
							OrderItemSerialID	
						FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials OSI
						WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
						/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
						AND SerialId NOT IN(SELECT   DIS.SerialId
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
											WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId
																AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																				WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderId
																				AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN(2,3)))
										
						
															 )											
											)
						AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT DISI.Serial FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DISI WHERE DISI.DocItemSerialID = OSI.SerialId),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
											NOT IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DIS.Serial AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
													WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																		FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																		WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId
																		AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																						WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderId
																						AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1))																
																		)																
								)
						
						/*RZ14000628- End*/
					OPEN Crsr_Add_Serial
					FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO @SerialId, @ordItemSerialDescription, @OrderItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/*---------------------------------------*/
						/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
						DECLARE @Serial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@RootId INT = NULL,@OprType INT = NULL
						/*Find doc type via orderOpr*/
						SET @OprType = (SELECT TOP 1[OprType]
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr]  DO
											WHERE DO.[DocOprID]  = ((SELECT  
																		(SELECT TOP 1 Opr.DocOprId 
																			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr WHERE Opr.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID) 			 
																		FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																		WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))						
										)
						/*check doc type*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 1)/*Doc*/
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SELECT TOP 1 
								 @Serial = [Serial]	
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
							WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @SerialId
							SET @RootId = 0
							SET @SerialId = NULL
						/**/
						END
						ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 2 OR  ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)/*Draft Or Movement*/
						BEGIN
						/**/
							/*----------------*/
							SELECT TOP 1 
								 @Serial = [Serial]							
								,@RootId = [RootId]
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
							WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @SerialId
							SET @RootId = ISNULL(@RootId,0)
							IF(@RootId = 0)
							BEGIN
								SET @RootId = @SerialId
							END
							/*ELSE IF(@RootId <> 0)
							BEGIN
								SET @RootId = @RootId
							END*/
							/*----------------*/
						/**/
						END						 			
						
						/*RZ14000325- End*/
						
						INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItemSerials (
							ItemId,
							SerialId,
							[Description],
							WHId,
							UserId,	
							Month1,	
							FPId, 
							RegisterDate, 
							RegisterDatePersian, 
							AppType,
							/*ADBF951130*/
							LanguageID,
							FirstRegisterDatePersian,
							FirstRegisterUserId,
							FirstRegisterDate,
							/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
							[Serial],
							[RootId]
							/*RZ14000325*/)	
						VALUES( 
							@DocItemId,
							@SerialId,
							@ordItemSerialDescription,
							/*@WhID,*/
							ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
							@UserId, 
							@Month1, 
							@FPId, 
							GETDATE(), 
							@RegisterDatePersian, 
							@AppType,
							/*ADBF951130*/
							@LanguageID,
							@RegisterDatePersian,
							@UserId,
							GETDATE(),
							/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
							@Serial,
							@RootId
							/*RZ14000325*/)
		
						SET @docItemSerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*Insert records in lang table*/			
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
						(
							[DocItemSerialID]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[Description]
						)
						SELECT
							@docItemSerialId,
							OISLN.LanguageID,
							OISLN.[Description]
						FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials_Lang OISLN 
						WHERE  OISLN.OrderItemSerialID = @OrderItemSerialID
		
						FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO  @SerialId, @ordItemSerialDescription, @OrderItemSerialID
					/*---------------------------------------*/
					END
					CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
					DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
					/*----------------- End user cursor for insert serial in doc --------------------------------*/				
					/*------------------- tran3 -----------------------*/
					IF (@@ERROR > 0)
					BEGIN 
						SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItemSerial' 
						SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
						/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
						CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
						DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
					ROLLBACK TRAN 
					END		
					ELSE 
					COMMIT TRAN 
					/*------------------- tran3 -----------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, 
										  @ordIDescription,@ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
										  @ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
										  ,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
										  ,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
										  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
										  ,@ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			END
			/*--*/
			CLOSE crsr
			DEALLOCATE crsr
			/*--*/
			/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN 
				SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItems' 
				SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
			/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/			
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Wrhs
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Wrhs
		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14000226- End*/

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	Set @DraftId = 0
	Return @DraftId
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	Return @DraftId
END	
/*--*/
END











GO
-- [dbo].[WSspIssueDraftFromPreOrder]








/*RZ14011005*/
/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspIssueDraftFromPreOrder]	
(		
/*
	declare @InsertedDocId INT = 0, @InsertedDocNumber INT = 0
	exec WSspIssueDraftFromPreOrder  
	@PreOrderId = 3356,
	@DocDate = '14011005',
	@DocDateTime = '2022-12-26',
	@DocOprId = 2,
	@PordItemList = NULL,	
	@UserId = 3,
	@Month1 = 10,
	@FPId = 1,
	@RegisterDate = '2022-12-26',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14011005',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,
	@InsertedDocId = @InsertedDocId OUTPUT,
	@InsertedDocNumber = @InsertedDocNumber OUTPUT
	 
	 select @InsertedDocId,@InsertedDocNumber
*/
@PreOrderId INT,
@DocDate NVARCHAR(8),
@DocDateTime DATETIME,
@DocOprId INT,
@PordItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
@InsertedDocId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedDocNumber INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------------- Set PordItemList -------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/		
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0) /*PreOrder not have any doc*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								pordIQuantity [Value]			
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID	
							/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
							AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
											  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
											  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
							/*RZ14020606- End*/
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	ELSE /*IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0)*/ /*PreOrder have doc*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0) - 
								ISNULL((SELECT SUM(Quantity) 
										FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI
										WHERE DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId 
														 FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
														 WHERE D.PreOrderId = POI.PreOrderId
														 )
										/*AND WHId = pordIWHId
										AND PrdId = pordIPrdId*/
										/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
										AND DI.PreOrderItemId = POI.PreOrderItemID
										/*RZ14021202- End*/
														 ),0)
	
								) [Value]				
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID	
							/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
							AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
											  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
											  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
							/*RZ14020606- End*/
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*���� ���� ���� � ��� �ǘ��� ����� �� ����� ���� �ѐ� ����� ���� ����  �� ������ ������ ����� �����  ����� �� ���.
 ���� �� ���� �ѐ� ����� �� ����� ������ ���� �� ���  �ǘ��� ���� ���.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0)) 
			FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems 
			WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
			/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
			AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
			/*RZ14020606- End*/
			),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(Quantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)),0)
	),0) = 0
)
BEGIN
	SELECT -1 AS 'Result'
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldDraftID INT,@DocId INT,@DocNumber INT,
@Description NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@SendAddress NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,@CompanyId INT = NULL,@CompanyPersonId INT = NULL,@ProjectId INT = NULL
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @actMovePordDescriptionIIToDoc INT
IF EXISTS (
SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromPreOrder' )
AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actMovePordDescriptionIIToDoc'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromPreOrder' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @actMovePordDescriptionIIToDoc = 1
ELSE
	SET @actMovePordDescriptionIIToDoc = 0
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP(1)
	@CompanyId = pordCompanyId,
	@CompanyPersonId = pordCompanyPersonId,
	@ProjectId = pordProjectId,
	@Description = (CASE WHEN(@actMovePordDescriptionIIToDoc = 1)
						THEN [pordDescriptionII]
						ELSE NULL
					END),
	@SendAddress = (ISNULL((SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = (SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = pordCompanyId)),'') 
					+ ' ' +
					ISNULL((SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = (SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = pordCompanyId)),'') 
					+ ' ' +
					(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = pordCompanyId))	
FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
WHERE PO.PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @WareHouseId INT
	DECLARE Crsr_Wrhs CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			pordiWHId
		FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
		WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId 		
		AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT JS.[Id] 
							  FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@PordItemList,'[]'))
							  WITH(
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'							  
							  ) JS
							  WHERE ISNULL(JS.[Value],0) > 0)
		/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
		AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
							WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
		/*RZ14020606- End*/
	OPEN Crsr_Wrhs
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN		
		SET @DocId = NULL
		SET @InsertedDocId = NULL
		SET @DocNumber = NULL
		SET @InsertedDocNumber = NULL
		/*---------------- Doc Number ----------------*/
		SELECT @DocNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(@DocOprId)
		SET @InsertedDocNumber = @DocNumber
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------- tran1 ----------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*--------------------------------------------*/		
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc]
				([OprId]
				,[DocNumber]
				,[DocDate]
				,[Description]
				,[IsFix]
				,[WHId]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[UserId]
				,[Month1]
				,[FPId]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[SendAddress]
				,[DocDateTime]
				,[CompanyId]
				,[CompanyPersonId]
				,[ProjectId]
				,[AppType]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[PreOrderId])
			VALUES
				(@DocOprId
				,@DocNumber
				,@DocDate
				,@Description
				,1/*IsFix*/
				,@WareHouseId
				,@UserId
				,@UserId
				,@Month1
				,@FPId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@SendAddress
				,@DocDateTime
				,@CompanyId
				,@CompanyPersonId
				,@ProjectId
				,@AppType
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@RegisterDate
				,@PreOrderId)

			SELECT @DocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

			IF(ISNULL(@DocId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
					(
					   [DocID]
					   ,[LanguageID]
					   ,[SendAddress]
					   ,[Description]
					 )
				SELECT 
					@DocId
					,POLN.LanguageID
					,((SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = (SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId) AND CYLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID) 
					+ ' '+
					(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = (SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID) 
					+ ' '+
					(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = @CompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
					)
					,(CASE WHEN(@actMovePordDescriptionIIToDoc = 1)
							THEN POLN.[pordDescriptionII]
							ELSE NULL
						END)
				FROM dbo.WSPreOrder_Lang POLN 
				WHERE POLN.PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
				AND POLN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
			END
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSDoc' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		SET @InsertedDocId = @DocId
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@DocId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @PreOrderItemID INT = NULL, @pordIPrdId INT = NULL, @pordIQuantity float = NULL 
			,@pordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @pordIWHId INT = NULL
			,@pordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@pordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL
			,@pordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
			,@pordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL
			,@pordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL
			,@pordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL
			,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT  = NULL
			, @TmpI FLOAT = NULL
			,@pordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
			BEGIN TRAN
			/*---------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE CrsrPordDocItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
			/**/
				SELECT 
					 [PreOrderItemID]
					,[pordIPrdId]
					,[pordIQuantity]
					,[pordIDescription]
					,[pordIPrdUnitId]
					,[pordIFCR]
					,[pordIFCRI]
					,[pordITracing]
					,[pordIQuantityTotal]
					,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]	
					,[pordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
				WHERE [PreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
				AND pordIWHId = @WareHouseId
				AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
										FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
										WITH (
											[Id] INT '$.Id',
											[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
										) AS JS	
									)	
				/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
				AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
									WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
				/*RZ14020606- End*/
			ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrPordDocItem
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordDocItem INTO @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIDescription
												,@pordIPrdUnitId,@pordIFCR,@pordIFCRI,@pordITracing,@pordIQuantityTotal
												,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------------- WSDocItems --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/

				DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
				SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
								) AS JS	
								WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID)
				IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordDocItem INTO @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIDescription
														,@pordIPrdUnitId,@pordIFCR,@pordIFCRI,@pordITracing,@pordIQuantityTotal
														,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult	
														,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					CONTINUE
				/**/
				END
				ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrQty,0))
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @pordIQuantityTotal = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1)
					/**/
					END
					/**/
					SET @pordIQuantity = @CurrQty
				/**/
				END			
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
				SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
				SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
				SET @TmpI = NULL
				/*--------------*/			
				SELECT 				
					@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
					@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
					@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductId = @pordIPrdId
				/*--------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				/*----------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
				BEGIN
				/**/				
					SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1)

					IF(ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END			
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems]
					([DocId]
					,[PrdId]
					,[Quantity]
					,[Description]
					,[WHId]
					,[UserId]
					,[Month1]
					,[FPId]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[FCR]
					,[FCRI]
					,[Tracing]
					,[PrdUnitId]
					,[QuantityTotal]
					,[PrdUnitIdI]
					,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					,[PreOrderItemId]/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSDocItems*/

					)
				VALUES
					(@DocId
					,@pordIPrdId
					,@pordIQuantity
					,@pordIDescription
					,@WareHouseId
					,@UserId
					,@Month1
					,@FPId
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@pordIFCR
					,@pordIFCRI
					,@pordITracing
					,@pordIPrdUnitId
					,@pordIQuantityTotal
					,@pordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					,@PreOrderItemID /*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
					)

				DECLARE @DocItemId INT = NULL
				SELECT @DocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				IF(ISNULL(@DocItemId,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems_Lang (
						 [DocItemID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[Description]	
						,[PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					) 
					SELECT
						@DocItemId,
						POILN.LanguageID,
						POILN.pordIDescription,
						POILN.pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang] POILN 
					WHERE  POILN.PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemID
					AND POILN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
				/**/
				END			
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordDocItem INTO @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIDescription
												,@pordIPrdUnitId,@pordIFCR,@pordIFCRI,@pordITracing,@pordIQuantityTotal
												,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			END
			CLOSE CrsrPordDocItem
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPordDocItem
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN 
				SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItems' 
				SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Wrhs
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Wrhs
		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main	
	SET @InsertedDocId = 0
	SET @InsertedDocNumber = 0	
	SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
/**/
END	
/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END











GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspIssueDuplicatePreOrder]
/*
	DECLARE @InsertedPreOrderId INT = 0,
	@InsertedPordNumber BIGINT = 0,
	@InsertedPordNumberRevision BIGINT = 0,
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) =''
	exec WSspIssueDuplicatePreOrder		 
	@PreOrderId = 2481,
	@UserId = 3,
	@Month1 = 8,
	@RegisterDate = '2020-11-14',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '13990824',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	/**/
	@InsertedPreOrderId = @InsertedPreOrderId OUTPUT,
	@InsertedPordNumber = @InsertedPordNumber OUTPUT,
	@InsertedPordNumberRevision = @InsertedPordNumberRevision OUTPUT,
	@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT	
	
	SELECT @InsertedPreOrderId,@InsertedPordNumber,
	@InsertedPordNumberRevision	, @ErrorMessage						
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedPordNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
@InsertedPordNumberRevision BIGINT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT
SET @ErrorMessage = ''
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(10)
SET @RegisterTime = CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME)
SET @RegisterDay = DATEPART(dw,@RegisterDate)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/	
BEGIN TRY  
    		
	/*======================= Get pordNumberRevison ==========================*/			
	DECLARE @pordNumberRevision BIGINT,@FPId INT,@pordNumber BIGINT
	/*SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FPId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId)*/
	SELECT TOP 1 @FPId = [FPId] , @pordNumber = [pordNumber] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	SELECT @pordNumberRevision =  dbo.WSfnReturnPreOrderNumberRevision(@PreOrderId, @FPId)
	SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = @pordNumberRevision		
	SET @InsertedPordNumber = @pordNumber		
	/*=============================== PreOrder ==========================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 1 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @InsertedPordId INT
	BEGIN TRAN	
		/*RZ991216- Add pordNumRef*/
		DECLARE @pordNumRef NVARCHAR(150)
		IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actAutoCalcNumRef'
								AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmPreOrderDataEntry' ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
		BEGIN
		/**/	
			DECLARE @pordCompanyId INT,@pordProjectId INT, @pordReferUserId INT,@LanguageID INT,
			@PreOrderOprID INT,@pordTypeId INT/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/
			SET @LanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
			DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE ([pordnumRef] NVARCHAR(150))
			SELECT TOP 1
				 @pordCompanyId = [pordCompanyId]			
				,@pordProjectId = [pordProjectId]
				,@pordReferUserId = [pordReferUserId]	
				/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/				
				,@PreOrderOprID = [PreOrderOprID]
				,@pordTypeId = [pordTypeId]
				/*RZ14000116- End*/				
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
			WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId							
			INSERT INTO @TmpTable([pordnumRef])	
			EXEC dbo.WSspReturnPreOrderNumRef 
			@PordCompanyId = @pordCompanyId,
			@PordProjectId = @pordProjectId,
			@PordReferUserId = @pordReferUserId,
			@PordNumber = @pordNumber,
			@PordNumberRevision = @pordNumberRevision,
			/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/
			@PreOrderOprId = @PreOrderOprID,
			@PordTypeId = @pordTypeId,
			/*RZ14000116- End*/
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID 
			SET @pordNumRef =(SELECT TOP 1 pordNumRef FROM @TmpTable)
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ991216- End*/			
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
			([pordTitle]
			,[pordNumber]
			,[pordDate]
			,[pordCompanyId]
			,[pordCompanyPersonId]
			,[pordProjectId]
			,[pordSubject]
			,[pordItemCount]
			,[pordItemsAmount]
			,[pordInstallAmount]
			,[pordDiscount]
			,[pordSumAmount]
			,[pordTax]
			,[pordTaxIndex]
			,[pordCalculateTax]
			,[pordTotalAmount]
			,[pordTotalAmountStr]			
			,[pordVerifierRequestId]
			,[pordVerifiedRegisterDate]			
			,[pordStatusId]		
			,[pordGuarantyId]
			,[pordPayConditionId]
			,[pordDeliveryTimeId]
			,[pordCreditDurationId]
			,[pordSpecialWord]
			,[pordDescriptionI]
			,[pordDescriptionII]
			,[pordDescriptionId]
			,[pordPrintFormatId]
			,[pordRLogoId]
			,[pordLLogoId]
			,[pordSigningI]
			,[pordSigningII]
			,[pordSigningIII]
			,[pordAccountNumberI]
			,[pordAccountShabaNumberI]
			,[pordAccountNumberII]
			,[pordAccountShabaNumberII]
			,[pordAddress]
			,[pordTelephone]
			,[pordFax]
			,[pordWebSite]
			,[pordEmail]
			,[pordSignatureId]
			,[pordImportDate]
			,[pordFirstRegisterUserId]
			,[UserId]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[pordDay]
			,[pordTime]			   
			,[pordDiscountTitle]
			,[pordDiscountPercent]
			,[pordTransportation]
			,[pordReferUserId]
			,[pordRound]
			,[pordcmpTitle]
			,[pordDateTime]
			,[pordImportDateTime]
			,[pordDeliveryDate]
			,[pordDeliveryDateTime]
			,[pordDeliveryTime]
			,[PreOrderOprID]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[pordVerificationWorkFlowId]
			,[pordExpireDate]
			,[pordExpireDateTime]			   
			,[pordCurrencyUnitId]
			,[pordNumberRevision]
			/*,[pordNumberPreFix] 
			,[pordNumberPostFix]*//*RZ991216*/
			,[pordDeliveryTermId]
			,[pordParentPreOrderId]/*RZ990915- Add pordParentPreOrderId*/
			/*RZ991119- Add pordDeliveryEndDate */
			,[pordDeliveryEndDate]
			,[pordDeliveryEndDateTime]
			,[pordDeliveryEndTime]

				/*RZ14020518- Add fields*/
				,[pordIDescriptionI]

				)

				 @InsertedPordItemId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[pordIQuantityDesc]
				,[pordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
				,[pordIDiscountDesc]					
				/*RZ14020324- End*/
				/*RZ14020518- Add fields*/
				,[pordIDescriptionI]		
				/*RZ14020518- End*/
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
			WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemID

			/*=========================== PreOrderItemSpecification=======================*/
			DECLARE @PreOrderItemSpcflID INT,@Specification NVARCHAR(300),@ISFPId INT,
			@SpecificationI NVARCHAR(300),@SpecificationII NVARCHAR(300),@ISLanguageID INT
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE Crsr_Add_Item_Spec CURSOR LOCAL FOR
				SELECT
					 [PreOrderItemSpcflID]
					,[Specification]
					,[FPId]

					,[SpecificationII]

													 @SpecificationI,@SpecificationII,@ISLanguageID

					,@UserId

					,@ISFPId

			CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item_Spec
			DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item_Spec
		
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*============================================================================*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIPrice,@pordITotalPrice,@pordIDescription,	
										   @IFPId,@pordIQuantityDesc,@pordIDiscount,@pordIDiscountI,@pordIDiscountPercent,@pordIDiscountIPercent,

										   ,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
										   /*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
										   ,@pordIDiscountDesc,@pordIDeliveryTime,@pordIDeliveryDay,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
										   ,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,@pordIDeliveryEndTime,@pordIDeliveryEndDay		
										   /*RZ14020324- End*/
										   ,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr/*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
										   /*RZ14020518- Add fields*/
										    ,@pordIValidityExpireDate,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
										   ,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/										  				
										   ,@pordIDescriptionI			
										   /*RZ14020518- End*/
										   /*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
										   ,@pordICurrencyUnitIdI,@pordICurrencyUnitIRate,@pordIPriceCurrencyI
										   ,@pordITotalPriceCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountICurrencyI

										   ,@pordIPriceSumCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountSumCurrencyI,@pordIDiscountISumCurrencyI,@pordITaxSumCurrencyI			
										   /*RZ14030301- End*/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item

	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert PreOrderItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE

	/*=========================== PreOrderPayments ====================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 3 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @PreOrderPaymentId INT,@ImportDate NVARCHAR(8),@pordpayTypeId INT,
	@pordpayDocId INT,@pordpayDocTypeId INT,@pordpayAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50),@pordpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@pordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordpayDescription NVARCHAR(2000),@PFPId INT,
	@ImportDateTime DATETIME,@pordpayDocDateTime DATETIME,@PLanguageID INT,
	/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
	@pordpayDeActive BIT,@pordpayAmountTemp NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ14020129- End*/
	,@pordpayAccountNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
	/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
	,@pordpayDocDateTemp NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp DATETIME = NULL
	/*RZ14030129- End*/
	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_Pay CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT 
				 [PreOrderPaymentId]
				,[ImportDate]
				,[pordpayTypeId]
				,[pordpayDocId]
				,[pordpayDocTypeId]
				,[pordpayAmount]
				,[pordpayDocNumber]
				,[pordpayDocDate]
				,[pordpayDocAmount]
				,[pordpayDescription]
				,[FPId]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				,[pordpayDocDateTime]
				,[LanguageID]	
				/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
				,[pordpayDeActive]
				,[pordpayAmountTemp]				
				/*RZ14020129- End*/
				,[pordpayAccountNumber]/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,[pordpayDocDateTemp]
				,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]	
			WHERE [pordpayPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Pay
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Pay INTO @PreOrderPaymentId,@ImportDate,@pordpayTypeId,
										  @pordpayDocId,@pordpayDocTypeId,@pordpayAmount,
										  @pordpayDocNumber,@pordpayDocDate,
										  @pordpayDocAmount,@pordpayDescription,@PFPId,
										  @ImportDateTime,@pordpayDocDateTime,@PLanguageID,
										  /*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
										  @pordpayDeActive,@pordpayAmountTemp,
										  /*RZ14020129- End*/
										  @pordpayAccountNumber/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
										  /*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
										  ,@pordpayDocDateTemp,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp 
										  /*RZ14030129- End*/

		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @InsertedPordPayId INT
			
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
				([ImportDate]
				,[pordpayTypeId]
				,[pordpayPreOrderId]
				,[pordpayDocId]
				,[pordpayDocTypeId]
				,[pordpayAmount]
				,[pordpayDocNumber]
				,[pordpayDocDate]
				,[pordpayDocAmount]
				,[pordpayDescription]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisteDate]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				,[pordpayDocDateTime]				
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
				,[pordpayDeActive]
				,[pordpayAmountTemp]				
				/*RZ14020129- End*/
				,[pordpayAccountNumber]/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,[pordpayDocDateTemp]
				,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
				)
			VALUES
				 (@ImportDate
				,@pordpayTypeId
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@pordpayDocId
				,@pordpayDocTypeId
				,@pordpayAmount
				,@pordpayDocNumber
				,@pordpayDocDate
				,@pordpayDocAmount
				,@pordpayDescription
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@UserId
				,@PFPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@ImportDateTime
				,@pordpayDocDateTime
				,@AppType
				,@PLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
				,@pordpayDeActive
				,@pordpayAmountTemp				
				/*RZ14020129- End*/
				,@pordpayAccountNumber/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,@pordpayDocDateTemp
				,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
				)
			
			SET @InsertedPordPayId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
				([PreOrderPaymentId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordpayDescription])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedPordPayId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordpayDescription]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
			WHERE PreOrderPaymentId = @PreOrderPaymentId
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14020330- Set deactive payment for old preorder*/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
			SET pordpayDeActive = 1
			WHERE PreOrderPaymentId = @PreOrderPaymentId
			/*RZ14020330- End*/
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Pay INTO @PreOrderPaymentId,@ImportDate,@pordpayTypeId,
											  @pordpayDocId,@pordpayDocTypeId,@pordpayAmount,
											  @pordpayDocNumber,@pordpayDocDate,
											  @pordpayDocAmount,@pordpayDescription,@PFPId,
											  @ImportDateTime,@pordpayDocDateTime,@PLanguageID,
											  /*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
											  @pordpayDeActive,@pordpayAmountTemp,
											  /*RZ14020129- End*/
											  @pordpayAccountNumber/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
											  /*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
											  ,@pordpayDocDateTemp,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp 
										      /*RZ14030129- End*/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Pay
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Pay
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert PreOrderPayments' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN

	/*=============== CrmFilesDirectory ===========================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 5 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @FileDirectoryID INT,@FileDirectoryTypeId INT,
	@MasterId INT,@DetailId INT,@SubDetailId INT,@Title NVARCHAR(50),
	@FolderName NVARCHAR(50),@FileName NVARCHAR(50),
	@FilePath NVARCHAR(200),@CopyFromPath NVARCHAR(200),
	@DocDate NVARCHAR(8),@DocDay NVARCHAR(20),
	@DocTime NVARCHAR(15),@DocDateTime DATETIME,
	@TypeOprObjectId INT,@FDLanguageID INT,@FDYear1 SMALLINT,
	@OrderView INT,@TypeOprObjectIdI INT,@TypeOprObjectIdII INT
	

	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_File CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT 
				 [FileDirectoryID]
				,[FileDirectoryTypeId]
				,[MasterId]
				,[DetailId]
				,[SubDetailId]
				,[Title]
				,[FolderName]
				,[FileName]
				,[FilePath]
				,[CopyFromPath]				
				,[DocDate]
				,[DocDay]
				,[DocTime]
				,[DocDateTime]
				,[TypeOprObjectId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Year1]
				,[OrderView]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdI]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdII]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
			WHERE TypeOprObjectId = @PreOrderId
			AND FileDirectoryTypeId IN (SELECT FileDirectoryTypeID 
										FROM [dbo].[CrmFileDirectoryType]
										WHERE FileDirectoryTypeOprId = 10) /*PreOrder*/
			ORDER BY [OrderView]
		OPEN Crsr_Add_File
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_File INTO @FileDirectoryID,@FileDirectoryTypeId,
										   @MasterId,@DetailId,@SubDetailId,@Title,
										   @FolderName,@FileName,@FilePath,@CopyFromPath,
										   @DocDate,@DocDay,@DocTime,@DocDateTime,
										   @TypeOprObjectId,@FDLanguageID,@FDYear1,
										   @OrderView,@TypeOprObjectIdI,@TypeOprObjectIdII
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @InsertedFileId INT
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory]
				([FileDirectoryTypeId]
				,[MasterId]
				,[DetailId]
				,[SubDetailId]
				,[Title]
				,[FolderName]
				,[FileName]
				,[FilePath]
				,[CopyFromPath]
				,[UserId]
				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[DocDate]
				,[DocDay]
				,[DocTime]				
				,[DocDateTime]
				,[TypeOprObjectId]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[OrderView]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdI]
				,[TypeOprObjectIdII])
			VALUES
				(@FileDirectoryTypeId
				,@MasterId
				,@DetailId
				,@SubDetailId
				,@Title
				,@FolderName
				,@FileName
				,@FilePath
				,@CopyFromPath
				,@UserId
				,@FDYear1
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@DocDate
				,@DocDay
				,@DocTime			
				,@DocDateTime
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@AppType
				,@FDLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@OrderView
				,@TypeOprObjectIdI
				,@TypeOprObjectIdII)

			SET @InsertedFileId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
				([FileDirectoryID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Title])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedFileId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Title]
			FROM [dbo].[CrmFilesDirectory_Lang]
			WHERE FileDirectoryID = @FileDirectoryID
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_File INTO @FileDirectoryID,@FileDirectoryTypeId,
											   @MasterId,@DetailId,@SubDetailId,@Title,
											   @FolderName,@FileName,@FilePath,@CopyFromPath,
											   @DocDate,@DocDay,@DocTime,@DocDateTime,
											   @TypeOprObjectId,@FDLanguageID,@FDYear1,
											   @OrderView,@TypeOprObjectIdI,@TypeOprObjectIdII
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_File
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_File
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert CrmFilesDirectory' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 	
		
	/*RZ990915- Add pordParentPreOrderId and commission*/
	/*=============== WSPreOrderCommissions =====================*/	
	/*-------------------- TRAN 6 --------------------------*/
	DECLARE @PreOrderCommissionId INT,@CommDateTime DATETIME,
	@CommDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),@CommCmpId INT,
	@CommUserId INT,@CommBaseAmountDec NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommBaseAmountDecDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommBaseAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommRate FLOAT,@CommRateDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommAmountCurrUnId INT,@CommAmountCurrencyRate FLOAT,
	@CommEffectiveDateTime DATETIME,@CommEffectiveDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@CommPayTypeId INT,@CommStatusId INT,@CommDesc NVARCHAR(4000),
	@CommIsVerified BIT,@CommVerifierUserId INT,@CommVerifiedDateTime DATETIME,
	@CommVerifiedDatePersian VARCHAR(8),@CommIsPaid BIT,@CommIsPaidUserId INT,
	@CommIsPaidDateTime DATETIME,@CommIsPaidDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId INT,@CoYear1 SMALLINT,@CoLanguageID INT

	BEGIN TRAN						
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_Comm CURSOR LOCAL FOR	
			SELECT 
				 [PreOrderCommissionId]
				,[CommDateTime]
				,[CommDatePersian]				
				,[CommCmpId]
				,[CommUserId]
				,[CommBaseAmountDec]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommBaseAmount]
				,[CommRate]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommAmount]
				,[CommAmountCurrUnId]
				,[CommAmountCurrencyRate]
				,[CommEffectiveDateTime]
				,[CommEffectiveDatePersian]
				,[CommPayTypeId]
				,[CommStatusId]
				,[CommDesc]
				,[CommIsVerified]
				,[CommVerifierUserId]
				,[CommVerifiedDateTime]
				,[CommVerifiedDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaid]
				,[CommIsPaidUserId]
				,[CommIsPaidDateTime]
				,[CommIsPaidDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId]				
				,[Year1]				
				,[LanguageID]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
			WHERE [CommPreOrderId] = @PreOrderId		
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Comm
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Comm INTO @PreOrderCommissionId,@CommDateTime,@CommDatePersian,@CommCmpId,
										   @CommUserId,@CommBaseAmountDec,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc,@CommBaseAmount,
										   @CommRate,@CommRateDesc,@CommAmount,@CommAmountCurrUnId,@CommAmountCurrencyRate,
										   @CommEffectiveDateTime,@CommEffectiveDatePersian,
										   @CommPayTypeId,@CommStatusId,@CommDesc,@CommIsVerified,@CommVerifierUserId,@CommVerifiedDateTime,
										   @CommVerifiedDatePersian,@CommIsPaid,@CommIsPaidUserId,
										   @CommIsPaidDateTime,@CommIsPaidDatePersian,
										   @CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId,@CoYear1,@CoLanguageID
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @InsertedCommId INT
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
				([CommDateTime]
				,[CommDatePersian]
				,[CommPreOrderId]
				,[CommCmpId]
				,[CommUserId]
				,[CommBaseAmountDec]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommBaseAmount]
				,[CommRate]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommAmount]
				,[CommAmountCurrUnId]
				,[CommAmountCurrencyRate]
				,[CommEffectiveDateTime]
				,[CommEffectiveDatePersian]
				,[CommPayTypeId]
				,[CommStatusId]
				,[CommDesc]
				/*,[CommIsVerified]
				,[CommVerifierUserId]
				,[CommVerifiedDateTime]
				,[CommVerifiedDatePersian]*/
				,[CommIsPaid]
				,[CommIsPaidUserId]
				,[CommIsPaidDateTime]
				,[CommIsPaidDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[UserId]
				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID])
			VALUES
				(@CommDateTime
				,@CommDatePersian
				,@InsertedPordId
				,@CommCmpId
				,@CommUserId
				,@CommBaseAmountDec
				,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc
				,@CommBaseAmount
				,@CommRate
				,@CommRateDesc
				,@CommAmount
				,@CommAmountCurrUnId
				,@CommAmountCurrencyRate
				,@CommEffectiveDateTime
				,@CommEffectiveDatePersian
				,@CommPayTypeId
				,@CommStatusId
				,@CommDesc
				/*,@CommIsVerified
				,@CommVerifierUserId
				,@CommVerifiedDateTime
				,@CommVerifiedDatePersian*/
				,@CommIsPaid
				,@CommIsPaidUserId
				,@CommIsPaidDateTime
				,@CommIsPaidDatePersian
				,@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@CoYear1
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@AppType
				,@CoLanguageID)

			SET @InsertedCommId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
				([PreOrderCommissionId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommDesc])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedCommId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommDesc]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
			WHERE [PreOrderCommissionId] = @PreOrderCommissionId

			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Comm INTO @PreOrderCommissionId,@CommDateTime,@CommDatePersian,@CommCmpId,
											   @CommUserId,@CommBaseAmountDec,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc,@CommBaseAmount,
										       @CommRate,@CommRateDesc,@CommAmount,@CommAmountCurrUnId,@CommAmountCurrencyRate,
										       @CommEffectiveDateTime,@CommEffectiveDatePersian,
										       @CommPayTypeId,@CommStatusId,@CommDesc,@CommIsVerified,@CommVerifierUserId,@CommVerifiedDateTime,
										       @CommVerifiedDatePersian,@CommIsPaid,@CommIsPaidUserId,
										       @CommIsPaidDateTime,@CommIsPaidDatePersian,
										       @CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId,@CoYear1,@CoLanguageID
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Comm
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Comm
		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	


	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN SET @ErrorMessage +=',Error In Insert WSPreOrderCommissions' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*RZ990915- End*/

	/*=========================================================================*/	
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14010531- Add actInsertDupPordWithOldPrice and add pordIQuantityTotal*/
	IF(ISNULL(@HavePermissionInsertDupPordWithOldPrice,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		SET @LangId = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
		IF(ISNULL(@LangId,0) = 0) 
			SET @LangId = 1065
		EXEC dbo.WSspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts @InsertedPordId,@Month1,@LangId,@LangId	
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*RZ14010531- End*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*=========================================================================*/		
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SET @InsertedPreOrderId = @InsertedPordId
		SET @InsertedPordNumber = @pordNumber
		SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = @pordNumberRevision
		SELECT 1					
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
		SET @InsertedPordNumber = 0
		SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = 0
		 
		SELECT 0			
	/**/
	END	
END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	/*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
    SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
	SET @InsertedPordNumber = 0
	SET @InsertedPordNumberRevision = 0
	
	SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END













GO
