					 [BordItemId]
					,[PrdQty]
					,[PrdPrice]
					,[PrdPriceGroupId]
				FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@BordItemList,'[]'))
				WITH(
					BordItemId INT '$.BordItemId',
					PrdQty FLOAT '$.PrdQty',
					PrdPrice NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdPrice',
					PrdPriceGroupId INT '$.PrdPriceGroupId'				
				)
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrOrdItem
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @BordItemId,@ordIQuantity,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,0) <= 0)
				BEGIN
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @BordItemId,@ordIQuantity,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
					CONTINUE
				END
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL,@ordIPrdId INT = NULL,@ItemCount INT=NULL,				
				@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
				@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL, @ordIOrderView INT = NULL,
				@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIWHId INT = NULL,		
				@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
				@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult  FLOAT = NULL,
				@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,@ordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
				@ordIDiscountI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountIPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,				
				@ordINotVisible BIT = NULL,@ordINotInSum BIT = NULL,@ordIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountISum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordILinkAccWHIdStr NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@ordIPrdColorId INT = NULL,
				@BuyOrderItemIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
				@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
				@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
				/*-------*/
				SET @ordITax = NULL
				SET @ordITaxSum = NULL
				SET @BuyOrderItemIds = CAST(@BordItemId AS NVARCHAR(50))
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SELECT  TOP(1)				
					 @ordIPrdId = [bordIPrdId]					
					,@ordIDescription = [bordIDescription]					
					,@ordIWHId = [bordIWHId]										
					,@ordIFCR = [bordIFCR]
					,@ordIFCRI = [bordIFCRI]					
					,@ordITracing = [bordITracing]
					,@ordIPrdUnitId = [bordIPrdUnitId]
					,@ordIQuantityTotal = [bordIQuantityTotal]
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI = [bordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult = [bordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,@ordIPrdNickname = [bordIPrdNickname]
					,@ordIQuantityDesc = [bordIQuantityDesc]
					,@ordINotVisible = [bordINotVisible]
					,@ordINotInSum = [bordINotInSum]
					,@ordILinkAccWHIdStr = [bordILinkAccWHIdStr]
					,@ordIPrdColorId = [bordIPrdColorId]
				FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
				WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] = @BordItemId
				/*-------------------*/
				SELECT 				
					@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
					@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
					@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductId = @ordIPrdId
				/*------------------*/
				SELECT TOP(1)
					@ordIDiscount = [Discount],
					@ordIDiscountPercent = [DiscountPrecent]
				FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice] PP
				WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId 
				AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ordIPrdPriceGroupID
				ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC,PrdPriceID DESC					
				/*------------------*/
				/*------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @ordIQuantity * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
				/*--------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				/*--------------*/
				IF (ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,1) 					
					/**/
					END
					ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)					
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*--------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)

					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
					SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100					
				/**/
				END				
				ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/	
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) * 100) / (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
						/*SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100*/
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				SET @TmpPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)			
				SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@TmpIII,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordITaxSum = ((ISNULL(@TmpIII,0)) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
						SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SET @ordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
				SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPrice))
					SET @ordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscount))					
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))			
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
					SET @ordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITotalPrice))					
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @InsertedOrderID)
				SET @ordIOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 4 ) - 1
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					([OrderId]
					,[ordIPrdId]
					,[ordIQuantity]
					,[ordIPrice]
					,[ordITotalPrice]
					,[ordIDescription]
					,[UserId]
					,[FPId]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[ordIQuantityDesc]
					,[ordIDiscountI]
					,[ordIDiscount]
					,[ordIDiscountPercent]
					,[ordIDiscountIPercent]
					,[ordIPrdUnitId]
					,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
					,[ordIOrderView]
					,[ordIFCR]
					,[ordITax]
					,[ordITaxIndex]
					,[ordICalculateTax]
					,[ordINotVisible]
					,[ordIFCRI]
					,[ordINotInSum]
					,[ordIWHId]
					,[ordIRound]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[ordIRoundPlus]					
					,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
					,[ordITracing]
					,[ordIPriceSum]
					,[ordIDiscountISum]
					,[ordIDiscountSum]
					,[ordITaxSum]					
					,[ordILinkAccWHIdStr]					
					,[ordIQuantityTotal]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[ordIPrdColorId]					
					,[ordIPrdNickname]															
					,[BuyOrderItemIds])
				VALUES
					(@InsertedOrderID
					,@ordIPrdId
					,@ordIQuantity
					,@ordIPrice
					,@ordITotalPrice
					,@ordIDescription
					,@UserId
					,@FPId
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@ordIQuantityDesc
					,@ordIDiscountI
					,@ordIDiscount
					,@ordIDiscountPercent
					,@ordIDiscountIPercent					
					,@ordIPrdUnitId
					,@ordCurrencyUnitId
					,@ordIOrderView
					,@ordIFCR
					,@ordITax
					,@ordITaxIndex
					,@ordICalculateTax
					,@ordINotVisible
					,@ordIFCRI
					,@ordINotInSum
					,@ordIWHId
					,@ordIRound
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@ordIRoundPlus					
					,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
					,@ordITracing					
					,@ordIPriceSum
					,@ordIDiscountISum
					,@ordIDiscountSum
					,@ordITaxSum					
					,@ordILinkAccWHIdStr
					,@ordIQuantityTotal
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@ordIPrdColorId					
					,@ordIPrdNickname										
					,@BuyOrderItemIds)

				SET @OrderItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemID,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
						([OrderItemID]
						,[ordIDescription]
						,[ordIQuantityDesc]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordIPrdNickname] 
						)
					SELECT
						 @OrderItemID
						,[bordIDescription]
						,[bordIQuantityDesc]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[bordIPrdNickname] 
					FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]
					WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] = @BordItemId
					AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID					
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL,@SerialId INT = NULL,
					@OrdItemSerialDesc NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,@OrdItemSerialWHId INT = NULL,
					@DocItemSerialID INT = NULL	,@BuyOrderItemSerialID INT = NULL
					/*------------ Create tmp table for serial ------*/
					DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
					DECLARE @Tmp_BordItemSerial TABLE(
						 [BuyOrderItemSerialID] INT
						,[Serial] NVARCHAR(50)
						,[Description] NVARCHAR(300)
						,[WHId] INT
						,[SerialId] INT										
					)
					DELETE FROM @Tmp_BordItemSerial
					/*--------------------*/
					IF(@OprType = 1) /*sale*/
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @Qry = N'
							SELECT 
	 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
	,[Serial]
	,[Description]
	,[WHId]
	,[SerialId]
FROM(
	SELECT  DISTINCT TOP ((CAST(' + CAST(@ordIQuantity AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AS INT))) 
		 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
		,[Serial]
		,[Description]
		,[WHId]
	   ,(SELECT TOP(1) [DocItemSerialID]
		FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
		WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
						   FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
						   WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
						   AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
						   AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
										  FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
										  WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')	
						   AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
							   = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
							)								
	
		) SerialId    
    
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials] BIS 
	WHERE BIS.[BuyOrderItemId] = ' + CAST(@bordItemId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '  
	AND ISNULL(Serial,'''') <> ''''
	AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
												IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
														FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
														WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																			FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
																			WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																			AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
																			AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																							FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																							WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																							/*AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1)*/)																
																			)																
													)
) AS TmpSerial '
						
						SET @Qry = @Qry + ' WHERE ISNULL([SerialId],0) > 0	
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) NOT IN(SELECT ISNULL(SerialId,0) 
															FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
															WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																						WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																											FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																											WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																	WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '))))
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) NOT IN(SELECT 
															DIS.[DocItemSerialID]
														FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS 
														INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
														INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
														WHERE D.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId			
														AND DI.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
														AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType IN(3))	/*IN(1,3)*/							
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT ISNULL(SerialId,0) 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
																						WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																												  WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																																	   FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																																	   WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																							  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																							  WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')))														 
																					)			
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT Isnull(DIS_I.SerialId, 0)
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS_I 
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI_I ON(DIS_I.ItemId = DI_I.DocItemID)
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D_I ON(DI_I.DocId = D_I.DocID)
																						WHERE D_I.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType = 3)
																						And DI_I.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '	 															
																						And D_I.WHIdI = TmpSerial.WHId	 
																						UNION
																						SELECT Isnull(DIS_II.SerialId, 0)
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS_II 
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI_II ON(DIS_II.ItemId = DI_II.DocItemID)
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D_II ON(DI_II.DocId = D_II.DocID)
																						WHERE D_II.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType = 2)
																						And DI_II.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 																
																						And D_II.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId 
																						)
							)
												'						
					/**/
					END
					ELSE IF(@OprType = 2) /*Return from sale*/
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @Qry = N'
							SELECT 
							 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
							,[Serial]
							,[Description]
							,[WHId]
							,[SerialId]
						FROM(
							SELECT  DISTINCT TOP((CAST(' + CAST(@ordIQuantity AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AS INT)))
								 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
								,[Serial]
								,[Description]
								,[WHId]
							   ,(SELECT TOP(1) [DocItemSerialID]
								FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
								WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
												   FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
												   WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
												   AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
												   AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																  FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																  WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' )	
												   AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
													   = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
													)								
	
								) SerialId    
    
							FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials] BIS 
							WHERE BIS.[BuyOrderItemId] = ' + CAST(@bordItemId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '  
							AND ISNULL(Serial,'''') <> ''''
							AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
																		IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
																				FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
																				WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																									FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
																									WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																									AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
																									AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																													FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																													WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																													/*AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1)*/)																
																									)																
																			)
						) AS TmpSerial ' 
						SET @Qry = @Qry + ' WHERE ISNULL([SerialId],0) > 0	
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) NOT IN(SELECT ISNULL(SerialId,0) 
															FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
															WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																						WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																											FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																											WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																	WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')))) 
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) IN(SELECT 
														DIS.[DocItemSerialID]						
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS 
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
													WHERE 
														D.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId			
														AND DI.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 			
														AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType IN(3))/*IN(1,3)*/
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT SerialId
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
																						WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																												  WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																																	   FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																																	   WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																								FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																								WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '))))
			
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT Isnull(DIS_I.SerialId, 0)
																					FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS_I 
																					INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI_I ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
																					INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D_I ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
																					WHERE D_I.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType = 2)
																					AND DI.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 				
																					AND D.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId	 
																					)
							)
						
						'						
					/**/
					END

					INSERT INTO @Tmp_BordItemSerial ([BuyOrderItemSerialID],[Serial],[Description],[WHId],[SerialId])
					EXECUTE(@Qry)
					/*-------------------------------------------*/

					DECLARE CrsrORDItemSerial CURSOR LOCAL FOR
					/**/	
						SELECT TOP (CAST(@ordIQuantity AS INT))
							 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
							,[Description]
							,[WHId]
							,[SerialId]
						FROM @Tmp_BordItemSerial
					/**/
					OPEN CrsrORDItemSerial
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @BuyOrderItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = NULL
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials]
							([OrderItemId]
							,[SerialId]
							,[Description]
							,[UserId]
							,[Month1]
							,[FPId]
							,[RegisterDate]
							,[RegisterDatePersian]							
							,[WHId]
							,[AppType]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							,[FirstRegisterDate])
						VALUES
							(@OrderItemId
							,@DocItemSerialID
							,@OrdItemSerialDesc
							,@UserId
							,@Month1
							,@FPId
							,@RegisterDate
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@OrdItemSerialWHId
							,@AppType
							,@DefaultLanguageID
							,@UserId
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@RegisterDate)
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*-------------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemSerialID,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang]
								([OrderItemSerialID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description])
							SELECT
								 @OrderItemSerialID
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description]
							FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials_Lang]
							WHERE BuyOrderItemSerialID = @BuyOrderItemSerialID
							AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID							
						/**/
						END
						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
					/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @BuyOrderItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					END
					CLOSE CrsrORDItemSerial
					DEALLOCATE CrsrORDItemSerial
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/							
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @BordItemId,@ordIQuantity,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			END
			CLOSE CrsrOrdItem
			DEALLOCATE CrsrOrdItem
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrdersItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 	
			SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
			SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
			ROLLBACK TRAN 			
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
			@OrderId = @InsertedOrderID,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID						
			/**/
			SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END

	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueOrdFromBord	
		SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
		SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
		SET @FinalResult = 0	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION MainIssueOrdFromBord
		IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
		END
		SET @FinalResult = 1	
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueOrdFromBord	
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @FinalResult AS 'Result'
/*--------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[WSspIssueOrderFromDoc]

BEGIN

,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,@ordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL

SEt @ordIsFixed = 1

	,@ordAccountShabaNumberI = [AccountShabaNumberI]
	,@ordAccountNumberII = [AccountNumberII]
	,@ordAccountShabaNumberII = [AccountShabaNumberII]	
	,@ordNote = ISNULL(@OrderNote,[Note])

	,@ordDefPayTypeId = [DefPayTypeId]
	,@ordSettlementTypeId = ISNULL(@OrderSettlementTypeId,SettlementTypeId)	
	/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
	,@ordITaxIndex = [TaxIndexItem]
    ,@ordICalculateTax = [CalculateTaxItem]
	/*RZ14021203- End*/
	,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI = [CurrencyUnitIdI]/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
FROM [dbo].[WSOrderDefaultSetting]
WHERE [OrderSettingID] =@OrderDefaultSettingId



/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP(1)
	@ordCompanyId = [CompanyId],
	@ordCompanyPersonId = [CompanyPersonId],

FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D
WHERE DocID = @DocId
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @ordNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnOrderNumber(@FPId, @OrderOprId)
SET @InsertedOrderNumber = @ordNumber

/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/

SET @ordDailyCounter = (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter](@OrderOprId, @ordDateTime))
/*RZ14011109-End*/
/*RZ14020324- Add ordDefaultSettingId*/

IF(ISNULL(@OrderDefaultSettingId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ordDefaultSettingId = @OrderDefaultSettingId

END
/*RZ14020324- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ordCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
								  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
										= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								  )
/**/
END
/**/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @ordCurrencyUnitId)
/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ordCurrencyUnitIRate = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyRate]
									FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnitRate] CUR

									AND CUR.[SrcCurrencyUnitId] = @ordCurrencyUnitId
									ORDER BY [RateDate] DESC)
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*RZ14030305- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOldOrderID,0) > 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT -1 AS 'Result'
	SET @InsertedOrderID = @InsertedOldOrderID
	SEt @InsertedOrderNumber = (SELECT TOP(1) ordNumber FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]  WHERE OrderId = @InsertedOldOrderID)
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT -2 AS 'Result'
	SET @InsertedOrderID = @BuyOrderId
	SEt @InsertedOrderNumber = (SELECT TOP(1) bordNumber FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]  WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@DocItemList,'') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemList)) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT -2 AS 'Result'
	SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
	SEt @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
/**/
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRAN
	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder]
        ([OrderOprID]
        ,[PreOrderId]
        ,[PreOrderVerifierId]
        ,[ordTitle]
        ,[ordNumber]
        ,[ordDate]
        ,[ordCompanyId]
        ,[ordCompanyPersonId]
        ,[ordProjectId]
        ,[ordSubject]        
        ,[ordTaxIndex]
        ,[ordCalculateTax]
        ,[ordIsVerified]
        ,[ordVerifiedRegisterDate]
        ,[ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
        ,[ordStatusId]
        ,[ordIsFixed]
        ,[ordGuarantyId]
        ,[ordSpecialWord]
        ,[ordDescriptionI]
        ,[ordDescriptionII]        
        ,[ordPrintFormatId]
        ,[ordRLogoId]
        ,[ordLLogoId]
        ,[ordSigningI]
        ,[ordSigningII]
        ,[ordSigningIII]
        ,[ordAccountNumberI]
        ,[ordAccountShabaNumberI]
        ,[ordAccountNumberII]
        ,[ordAccountShabaNumberII]
        ,[ordSignatureId]
        ,[ordImportDate]
        ,[ordFirstRegisterUserId]
        ,[UserId]
        ,[FPId]
        ,[Month1]
        ,[RegisterDate]
        ,[RegisterDatePersian]

        ,[ordDay]

        ,[ordReferUserId]

        ,[ordDateTime]
        ,[ordImportDateTime]
        ,[ordSerial]
        ,[AppType]
        ,[ordVerifierRequestId]
        ,[LanguageID]
        ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
        ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
        ,[FirstRegisterDate]
        ,[ordVerificationWorkFlowId]
        ,[ordCurrencyUnitId]
        ,[ordDeliveryTermId]       
        ,[ordDefWHId]
        ,[ordDefPrdPriceGroupId]
        ,[ordSellerId]
        ,[ordEmailUsersInfoId]
        ,[ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId]
        ,[ordSaleCenterId]
        ,[ordDefPayTypeId]
		,[ordDailyCounter]/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
		,[ordDefaultSettingId]/*RZ14020324- Add ordDefaultSettingId*/
		,[ordTspSettingId] /*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
		/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
		,[ordCurrencyUnitIdI]
		,[ordCurrencyUnitIRate]
		/*RZ14030305- End*/
		)
    VALUES
        (@OrderOprID
        ,@PreOrderId
        ,@PreOrderVerifierId
        ,@ordTitle
        ,@ordNumber
        ,@ordDate
        ,@ordCompanyId
        ,@ordCompanyPersonId
        ,@OrderProjectId
        ,@ordSubject        
        ,@ordTaxIndex
        ,@ordCalculateTax
        ,NULL/*ordIsVerified*/
        ,NULL /*ordVerifiedRegisterDate*/
        ,NULL/*ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian*/
        ,1/*ordStatusId*/
        ,@ordIsFixed
        ,@ordGuarantyId
        ,@ordSpecialWord
        ,@ordDescriptionI
        ,@ordDescriptionII        
        ,@OrderPrintFormatID
        ,@ordRLogoId
        ,@ordLLogoId
        ,@ordSigningI
        ,@ordSigningII
        ,@ordSigningIII
        ,@ordAccountNumberI
        ,@ordAccountShabaNumberI
        ,@ordAccountNumberII
        ,@ordAccountShabaNumberII
        ,@ordSignatureId
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@UserId
        ,@UserId
        ,@FPId
        ,@Month1
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,NULL/*ordVerifierId*/
        ,@ordSettlementTypeId
        ,@ordDay
        ,@ordTime                
        ,@OrderReferUserId        
        ,@ordNote
        ,@ordDateTime
        ,@RegisterDate        
        ,@ordSerial
        ,@AppType
        ,@ordVerifierRequestId
        ,@DefaultLanguageID
        ,@UserId
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@ordVerificationWorkFlowId        
        ,@ordCurrencyUnitId
        ,@ordDeliveryTermId                
        ,@ordDefWHId
        ,@ordDefPrdPriceGroupId
        ,@ordSellerId
        ,@ordEmailUsersInfoId
        ,@ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId
        ,@ordSaleCenterId
        ,@ordDefPayTypeId
		,@ordDailyCounter/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
		,@ordDefaultSettingId/*RZ14020324- Add ordDefaultSettingId*/
		,@ordTspSettingId/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
		/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
		,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI
		,@ordCurrencyUnitIRate
		/*RZ14030305- End*/
		)
	SET @InsertedOrderID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	SET @InsertedOrderNumber = @ordNumber

	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@OrderDefaultSettingId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
				([OrderID]
				,[ordTitle]
				,[ordSubject]
				,[ordSpecialWord]
				,[ordDescriptionI]
				,[ordDescriptionII]
				,[ordSigningI]
				,[ordSigningII]
				,[ordSigningIII]           
				,[ordNote]           
				,[LanguageID])
			SELECT 
				@InsertedOrderID
				,[Title]
				,[Subject]
				,[SpecialWord]
				,[DescriptionI]
				,[DescriptionII]
				,[SigningI]
				,[SigningII]
				,[SigningIII]           
				,ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Value] 
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@OrderNoteLang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[Value] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Value',
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId'
						) JS
						WHERE JS.LanguageId = SD.LanguageID						
						),[Note])
				,[LanguageID]
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang] SD
			WHERE [OrderSettingID] =@OrderDefaultSettingId 
			AND SD.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID		
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OrderDefaultSettingId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
				([OrderID]
				,[ordNote]           
				,[LanguageID])
			SELECT 
				@InsertedOrderID
				,[Value]
				,[LanguageId]
			FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@OrderNoteLang,'[]'))
			WITH(
				[Value] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Value',
				[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId'
			) JS
			WHERE JS.LanguageId <> @DefaultLanguageID		
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END

	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrders' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 		
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @DocItemId INT = NULL,@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL
		BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrOrdItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
			/**/				
				SELECT 
				*
				FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@DocItemList,'[]'))
				WITH(
					DocItemId INT '$.DocItemId',
					PrdPrice NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdPrice',
					PrdPriceGroupId INT '$.PrdPriceGroupId'				
				)
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrOrdItem
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @DocItemId,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL,@ordIPrdId INT = NULL,@ItemCount INT=NULL,
				@ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
				@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
				@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL, @ordIOrderView INT = NULL,
				@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIWHId INT = NULL,		
				@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
				@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
				@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/				
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SELECT  TOP(1)				
					 @ordIPrdId = [PrdId]
					,@ordIQuantity = [Quantity]					
					,@ordIDescription = [Description]					
					,@ordIWHId = [WHId]										
					,@ordIFCR = [FCR]
					,@ordIFCRI = [FCRI]					
					,@ordITracing = [Tracing]
					,@ordIPrdUnitId = [PrdUnitId]
					,@ordIQuantityTotal = [QuantityTotal]
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI]
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = [PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,@ordIPrdNickname = [PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPriceSum = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0)					
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)) * ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0)					
				END
				/**/
				/*SET @ordITotalPrice = @ordIPriceSum*/

				/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordITaxSum = ((ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0)) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
						SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)

					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				
				SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0) 

				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPrice))					
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))			
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
					SET @ordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITotalPrice))					
				END
				/*RZ14021203- End*/



				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @InsertedOrderID)
				SET @ordIOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 4 ) - 1
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					([OrderId]
					,[ordIPrdId]
					,[ordIQuantity]
					,[ordIPrice]
					,[ordITotalPrice]
					,[ordIDescription]
					,[UserId]
					,[FPId]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[ordIPrdUnitId]
					,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
					,[ordIOrderView]
					,[ordIFCR]
					,[ordIFCRI]
					,[ordIWHId]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
					,[ordITracing]
					,[ordIPriceSum]
					,[ordIQuantityTotal]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[ordIPrdNickname]/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
					,[ordITax] 
					,[ordITaxIndex] 
					,[ordICalculateTax] 
					,[ordITaxSum] 
					/*RZ14021203- End*/
					)
				VALUES
					(@InsertedOrderID
					,@ordIPrdId
					,@ordIQuantity
					,@ordIPrice
					,@ordITotalPrice
					,@ordIDescription
					,@UserId
					,@FPId
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@ordIPrdUnitId
					,@ordCurrencyUnitId
					,@ordIOrderView
					,@ordIFCR
					,@ordIFCRI
					,@ordIWHId
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
					,@ordITracing
					,@ordIPriceSum
					,@ordIQuantityTotal
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
					,@ordITax
					,@ordITaxIndex
					,@ordICalculateTax
					,@ordITaxSum
					/*RZ14021203- End*/
					)
				SET @OrderItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemID,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
						([OrderItemID]
						,[ordIDescription]						
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
						)
					SELECT
						 @OrderItemID
						,[Description]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]
					WHERE DocItemID = @DocItemId
					AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
					/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSDocItems]
					SET [OrderItemId] = @OrderItemID
					WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId
					/*RZ14021203- End*/
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL,@SerialId INT = NULL,
					@OrdItemSerialDesc NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,@OrdItemSerialWHId INT = NULL,
					@DocItemSerialID INT = NULL
					


					DECLARE CrsrORDItemSerial CURSOR LOCAL FOR
					/**/				
						SELECT 
							 [DocItemSerialID]
							,[Description]
							,[WHId]
							,[SerialId]							
						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
						WHERE [ItemId] = @DocItemId
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) <> 0
					/**/
					OPEN CrsrORDItemSerial
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @DocItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = NULL
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials]
							([OrderItemId]
							,[SerialId]
							,[Description]
							,[UserId]
							,[Month1]
							,[FPId]
							,[RegisterDate]
							,[RegisterDatePersian]							
							,[WHId]
							,[AppType]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							,[FirstRegisterDate])
						VALUES
							(@OrderItemId
							,@DocItemSerialID
							,@OrdItemSerialDesc
							,@UserId
							,@Month1
							,@FPId
							,@RegisterDate
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@OrdItemSerialWHId
							,@AppType
							,@DefaultLanguageID
							,@UserId
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@RegisterDate)
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*-------------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemSerialID,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang]
								([OrderItemSerialID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description])
							SELECT
								 @OrderItemSerialID
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description]
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
							WHERE DocItemSerialID = @DocItemSerialID
							AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID							
						/**/
						END

						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
					/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @DocItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					END
					CLOSE CrsrORDItemSerial
					DEALLOCATE CrsrORDItemSerial
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/							
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @DocItemId,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			END
			CLOSE CrsrOrdItem
			DEALLOCATE CrsrOrdItem
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrdersItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 	
			SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN

		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
			@OrderId = @InsertedOrderID,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc]
			SET OrderId = @InsertedOrderID
			WHERE DocID = @DocId
			/**/
			SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
		/**/
		END
		


		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END

	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
	SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
	SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
/**/
END	
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END








GO

Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspIssueOrderFromPreOrder]	
(

*/

@OrderReferUserId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50), 
/*950403*/
@ordNote NVARCHAR(4000)= NULL, 
@ordSettlementTypeId INT= NULL,
@OrderDefaultSettingId INT= NULL,  
/**/
@InsertedOrderID INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
/*ADBF951130*/
@LanguageID INT,
/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
@ordNoteLang NVARCHAR(MAX)= NULL,
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
@OrdProjectId INT = NULL,
@PordItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@IsTransferFreePay BIT = NULL,
@IsTransferFreeComm BIT = NULL,
/*RZ14010531- End*/
@ordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
/*RZ14020604- Add parameters*/
@ordIssueSettingByPrdId INT = NULL,
@ordIssueIndexedByPrdId INT = NULL
/*RZ14020604- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
IF(ISNULL(@OrdProjectId,0) = 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @OrdProjectId = (SELECT pordProjectId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO WHERE PO.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID)
/**/
END
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	/*---------- Issue one order ------------*/
	SET @IsTransferFreePay = 1
	SET @IsTransferFreeComm = 1
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) OrderId FROM WSOrder WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0) /*PreOrder not have any order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								pordIQuantity [Value],
								/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
								pordIValidityExpireDate [ValidityExpireDate],
								pordIValidityExpireDatePersian [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
								pordIPrdAutoCalcRate [PrdAutoCalcRate]
								/*RZ14020604- End*/
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID		
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	ELSE /*IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) OrderId FROM WSOrder WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) > 0)*/ /*PreOrder have order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0) - 
								ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ordIQuantity) 
										FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI
										WHERE OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId 
														 FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
														 WHERE O.PreOrderId = POI.PreOrderId
														 )
										/*AND ordIWHId = pordIWHId
										AND ordIPrdId = pordIPrdId
										AND ordIPrice = pordIPrice*/
										/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSOrderItems*/
										AND OI.PreOrderItemId = POI.PreOrderItemID
										/*RZ14021202- End*/
														 ),0)
	
								) [Value],
								/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
								pordIValidityExpireDate [ValidityExpireDate],
								pordIValidityExpireDatePersian [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
								pordIPrdAutoCalcRate [PrdAutoCalcRate]
								/*RZ14020604- End*/
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID								
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
		/*RZ14020709- Add condition*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') <>'')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
									 [Id]
									,[Value]
									,[ValidityExpireDate]
									,[ValidityExpireDatePersian]
									,[PrdAutoCalcRate]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	,
								[ValidityExpireDate] DATETIME '$.ValidityExpireDate',
								[ValidityExpireDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ValidityExpireDatePersian',
								[PrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdAutoCalcRate'			
								) AS JS	
								WHERE ISNULL([Value],0) > 0	
								FOR JSON PATH)
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14020709- End*/
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
/**/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderID))
/*RZ14010531- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/

/*SELECT @InsertedOldPreOrderID = OrderID FROM dbo.WSOrder Ord WHERE Ord.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
IF @InsertedOldPreOrderID > 0 RETURN -1*/
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
/*���� ���� ���� � ��� �ǘ��� ����� �� ����� ���� �ǘ��� ���� ����  �� ������ ������ ����� �����  ����� �� ���.
 ���� �� ���� �ǘ��� �� ����� ������ ���� �� ��� �ǘ��� ���� ���.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ordIQuantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O WHERE O.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID)),0)
	),0) <= 0 /* = 0*/

	OR ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = ''/*RZ14020709- Add condition change = 0*/
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END
/*RZ14010531- End*/

/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
DECLARE @OrdDateTime DATETIME, @TimeNow TIME

SET @TimeNow = CONVERT(TIME, GETDATE())
SET @OrdDateTime = @OrdDateTime + CAST(@TimeNow AS DATETIME)
/*RZ980703- End*/

/*RZ14011109-End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE
/*@Title VARCHAR(200)*/@Title NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
/*@Subject VARCHAR(300)*/@Subject NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
@GuarantyId INT,

/*@DescriptionI VARCHAR(300)*/@DescriptionI NVARCHAR(MAX), /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/

/*------------ for get record in lang table ---------*/

@OrderSettingID INT,

SELECT 
@Title = Title, @Subject = [Subject], @GuarantyId = GuarantyId, @TaxIndex = 
TaxIndex,

DescriptionII,

 /*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
 ,@OrderSettingID = OrderSettingID, @Note = Note
FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting
/*950304
WHERE UserId = @UserId
*/
WHERE ReferUserId = @UserId

/*---------------------------------------------------*/

						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueOrderFromPreOrder' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionMovePordDescIIToOrd = 1
ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionMovePordDescIIToOrd = 0

	/*-----------------------*/
	SELECT

		/*@DefsetTaxIndex = TaxIndex, 
		@DefsetCalculateTax = CalculateTax,*/ 
		@DefsetSpecialWord = SpecialWord,

				,@ordDailyCounter/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
				/*RZ14020210- Add pordDeadlineDate*/
				,pordDeadlineDate
				,pordDeadlineDatePersian
				/*RZ14020210- End*/
				/*RZ14020218- Add fields from orderDefaultsetting*/
				,@DefsetVerificationWorkFlowId
				,pordDefWHId
				,pordSellerId
				,pordEmailUsersInfoId
				,pordEmailUserDefaultSettingId			
				,pordDefPrdPriceGroupId
				/*RZ14020218- End*/
				/*RZ14020306- Add verify order after issue and not send payDeActive*/
				,@ordIsVerified
				,@ordVerifiedRegisterDate
				,@ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian
				,@ordVerifierId
				/*RZ14020306- End*/
				/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
				,@OrderDefaultSettingId      
				,[pordDeliveryDay]
				,[pordDeliveryEndDay]
				/*RZ14020324- End*/
				,@ordTspSettingId/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
				/*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
				,[pordCurrencyUnitIdI]
				,[pordCurrencyUnitIRate]
				/*RZ14030301- End*/
			FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
			WHERE preOrderID = @PreOrderID

			/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			/*RZ960218 dar inja nesbat dehi be @orderid anjam mishavad*/
			Select @OrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSOrder_Lang (
				[OrderID]
				,[ordTitle]
				,[ordSubject]
				,[ordSpecialWord]
				,[ordDescriptionI]
				,[ordDescriptionII]
				,[ordSigningI]
				,[ordSigningII]
				,[ordSigningIII]
				,[ordDiscountTitle]
				,[ordNote]
				,[ordcmpTitle]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[ordTotalAmountStr]
				)
			SELECT 
				@OrderId
				,ISNULL(
						(SELECT [Title] FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
						, 
	
						(SELECT [Title] FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @OrderSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
				)
				,ISNULL(
						(SELECT [Subject] FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
						, 
	
						(SELECT [Subject] FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @OrderSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
				)
				,ISNULL(
						(SELECT SpecialWord FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
						, 
	
						(SELECT SpecialWord FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @OrderSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
				)
				,ISNULL(
						(SELECT DescriptionI FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
						, 
	
						(SELECT DescriptionI FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @OrderSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
				)
				,ISNULL(
						(SELECT DescriptionII FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
						, 
	
						(SELECT DescriptionII FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @OrderSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
				)
	
				,(SELECT SigningI FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)	
				,(SELECT SigningII FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)	
				,(SELECT SigningIII FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)	
				,[pordDiscountTitle]
				,ISNULL(
						(SELECT dbo.CRMfnReturnValueInMultiLanguage(POLN.LanguageID, @ordNoteLang)),
						(SELECT [Note] FROM dbo.WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang ODSLN WHERE ODSLN.OrderSettingID = @DefsetSettingID AND ODSLN.LanguageID = POLN.LanguageID)
				)
				,[pordcmpTitle]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordTotalAmountStr]
			FROM dbo.WSPreOrder_Lang POLN
			WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderID
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/
		/**/
		END
	/*----------------------------------------*/
	END

	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrders' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	/*==============================================
	--==============================================
	--==============================================*/
	/*Declare @OrderId INT 
	Select @OrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()*/
	/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
	/*RZ960218 dalile jabejaie declare @orderId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be orderid jadid niaz darim*/
	/*chon dar zaman darj order be @orderId id jadid nesbat dade shode inja be 2 dastor bala niazi nist*/
	SET @InsertedOrderID = @OrderId
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------
	------------------- tran2-----------------------*/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/*------------------- tran2*/
	/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
	/*----------------- Begin user cursor for insert items in order -----------------------------*/
	/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @PreOrderItemID INT
	DECLARE @pordIPrdId INT
	DECLARE @pordIQuantity FLOAT
	/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
	DECLARE @pordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(MAX)
	/*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/
	DECLARE @pordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) 
	DECLARE @pordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2)
	DECLARE @pordIDiscountI NUMERIC(18,2)
	DECLARE @pordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ991008- End*/
	/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
	DECLARE @pordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX)
	DECLARE @pordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2)
	DECLARE @pordIDiscountIPercent NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/
	DECLARE @pordIRound NUMERIC(18,2)
	DECLARE @pordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ991008- End*/
	DECLARE @pordIPrdUnitId INT
	DECLARE @pordICurrencyUnitId INT
	DECLARE @pordIFCR FLOAT
	DECLARE @pordIFCRI FLOAT
	DECLARE @pordIOrderView INT
	DECLARE @pordITax NUMERIC(18,2)/*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/
	DECLARE @pordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2)
	DECLARE @pordICalculateTax BIT 
	DECLARE @pordINotVisible BIT
	DECLARE @pordINotInSum BIT
	DECLARE @pordIWHId INT
	DECLARE @ItemId INT
	/*ADBF990126*/
	DECLARE @pordIPrdPriceGroupID INT
	DECLARE @pordITracing NVARCHAR(200) /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
	/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
	DECLARE @pordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2)
	,@pordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2)
	,@pordIDiscountISum NUMERIC(18,2),@pordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ990815- End*/
	/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
	,@pordIDeliveryTermId INT,@pordIDeliveryDate DATETIME,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
	/*RZ990916- End*/
	 ,@pordIQuantityTotal FLOAT /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
	 ,@pordIPrdColorId INT/*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
	 /*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
	 ,@pordIPrdUnitIdI INT,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT
	 ,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT 
	 /*RZ14010620- End*/
	 ,@pordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
	/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
	,@pordIDiscountDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL 			
	/*RZ14020324- End*/
	,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr NVARCHAR(50) = NULL/*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
	/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
	,@pordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL
	,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/
	@pordIDescriptionI NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
	/*RZ14020510- End*/	
	/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
	Declare Crsr_Add_Item CURSOR For
		SELECT 
			PreOrderItemID,
			pordIPrdId, 
			pordIQuantity, 
			pordIQuantityDesc,
			pordIPrice, 
			pordIDiscount,
			pordIDiscountI,
			pordITotalPrice, 
			pordIDescription, 
			pordIDiscountPercent,
			pordIDiscountIPercent,
			pordIRound,
			pordIRoundPlus,
			pordIPrdUnitId,
			pordICurrencyUnitId,
			pordIFCR, 
			pordIFCRI,
			pordIOrderView,
			pordITax, 
			pordITaxIndex, 
			pordICalculateTax, 
			pordINotVisible, 
			pordINotInSum,
			pordIWHId
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, pordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
				,[pordIPriceSum]
				,[pordIDiscountSum]
				,[pordIDiscountISum]
				,[pordITaxSum]
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,[pordIDeliveryTermId],[pordIDeliveryDate],[pordIDeliveryDatePersian]
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			,[pordIQuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			,[pordIPrdColorId]/*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			,[pordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/	
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,[pordIDiscountDesc]
			,[pordIDeliveryTime]
			,[pordIDeliveryDay]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDate]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndTime]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDay] 			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			,[pordILinkAccWHIdStr]/*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
			,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
			/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/	
			,[pordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
			,[pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo]
			/*RZ14020528- End*/				
			,[pordIDescriptionI]
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
		FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
		WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
								) AS JS	
								/*WHERE ISNULL([Value],0) > 0*/)
		/*RZ14010531- End*/		
		ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	

	OPEN Crsr_Add_Item
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @PreOrderItemID, @pordIPrdId, @pordIQuantity, 
								@pordIQuantityDesc, @pordIPrice, @pordIDiscount, @pordIDiscountI,
								@pordITotalPrice, @pordIDescription, @pordIDiscountPercent, @pordIDiscountIPercent, 
								@pordIRound, @pordIRoundPlus, @pordIPrdUnitId, @pordICurrencyUnitId, @pordIFCR,
								@pordIFCRI, @pordIOrderView, @pordITax, @pordITaxIndex, @pordICalculateTax,
								@pordINotVisible, @pordINotInSum, @pordIWHId
								/*ADBF990126*/
								, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID,@pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
								,@pordIPriceSum,@pordIDiscountSum,@pordIDiscountISum,@pordITaxSum/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/								
								,@pordIDeliveryTermId,@pordIDeliveryDate,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
								,@pordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
								,@pordIPrdColorId /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
								,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/	
								,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
								/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
								,@pordIDiscountDesc,@pordIDeliveryTime,@pordIDeliveryDay,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
								,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,@pordIDeliveryEndTime,@pordIDeliveryEndDay 			
								/*RZ14020324- End*/
								,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
								/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
								,@pordIValidityExpireDate,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
								,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/								
								,@pordIDescriptionI
								/*RZ14020510- End*/


	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
		/*SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
						FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
						WITH (
							[Id] INT '$.Id',
							[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
						) AS JS	
						WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID)*/


		/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
		DECLARE @jsValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@jsValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
		@jsPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
		/**/
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			@CurrQty = [Value],
			@jsValidityExpireDate = [ValidityExpireDate],
			@jsValidityExpireDatePersian = [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
			@jsPrdAutoCalcRate = [PrdAutoCalcRate]
		FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
		WITH (
			[Id] INT '$.Id',
			[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	,
			[ValidityExpireDate] DATETIME '$.ValidityExpireDate',
			[ValidityExpireDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ValidityExpireDatePersian',
			[PrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdAutoCalcRate'			
		) AS JS	
		WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID	
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@HasActionCanChangeValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = @jsValidityExpireDate
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDatePersian = @jsValidityExpireDatePersian
		/**/
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@HasActionCanChangePrdAutoCalcRate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIPrdAutoCalcRate = @jsPrdAutoCalcRate
		/**/
		END
		/**/
		/*RZ14020604- End*/		

		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------- Calculate ------------------------*/
		DECLARE @ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIOrderView INT = NULL,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @ordICalculateTax BIT = NULL, 
		@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,
		@PordTmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ItemCount INT = NULL,
		@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ExtraCalcItem NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ExtraCalcItemPercent FLOAT = NULL
			

		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		SET @ordIPrice = @pordIPrice
		/*Set OrderView*/
		SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
		SET @ordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
		/**/
		SET @ordICalculateTax = @pordICalculateTax
		SET @ordIFCR = @pordIFCR
		SET @ordIFCRI = @pordIFCRI
		SET @ordIRound = @pordIRound
		SET @ordIRoundPlus = @pordIRoundPlus
		SET @ordITaxIndex = @pordITaxIndex
		/*------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) = 0 )
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIQuantity = @pordIQuantity
			SET @ordITotalPrice = @pordITotalPrice
			SET @ordIDiscount = @pordIDiscount
			SET @ordIDiscountPercent = @pordIDiscountPercent			
			SET @ordITax = @pordITax			
			SET @ordIPriceSum = @pordIPriceSum
			SET @ordIDiscountSum = @pordIDiscountSum
			SET @ordITaxSum = @pordITaxSum
			SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @pordIQuantityTotal 			
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <> 0 )
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIQuantity = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @ordIQuantity * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
			END
			/**/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
			SET @PordTmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)

			/*----------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL			
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @pordIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 
				SET @PordTmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) 
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
				SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)/*RZ14010628- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

				IF(ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			/*----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ExtraCalcItemPercent = (ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0) / (ISNULL(@PordTmpI,1)))
				SET @ordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@ExtraCalcItemPercent,0) * @TmpI
				SET @ordIDiscount = ISNULL(@ExtraCalcItemPercent,0)
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
					SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0)
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN					
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))
					SET @ordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscount))
					/*RZ14020626-End*/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @TmpPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)			
			SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0)	
			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@TmpIII,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
				SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0)) / 100	
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN	
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))
					/*RZ14020626- End*/
				END
			/**/
			END

			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @ordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
			SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN			
				/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
				SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
				SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@cuUnitIsDecimal))
				/*RZ14020626-End*/
			END 
		/**/
		END	
		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010531- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/

		/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
		DECLARE @ordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
		,@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,@IsCalcValidityExpireDate BIT = NULL
		,@PrdValidityTimeHour INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTimeMinute INT = NULL		
		SELECT  
			 @PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
			,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @pordIPrdId
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

		IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
			SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
			SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@ordIValidityExpireDate))
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14010319- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020406- Add ordIPrdValidityCounting*/
		DECLARE @ordIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL
		SELECT TOP (1) 					
				@ordIPrdValidityCountingNum = (CASE WHEN (ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive],0) = 1)
												THEN [PrdValidityCountingNum]
												ELSE NULL
											END)
			,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin = (CASE WHEN (ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive],0) = 1)
												THEN [PrdValidityCountingMin]
												ELSE NULL
											END)
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @pordIPrdId
		/*RZ14020406- End*/

								/*RZ14020324- End*/

	/**/
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item

	/*----------------- END user cursor for insert items in order -------------------------------*/

	/*------------------- tran2*/

	/*------------------- tran3*/

	/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @pordpayTypeId INT

	DECLARE @pordpayDocTypeId INT
	DECLARE @pordpayAmount NUMERIC(18,2) /*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/
	DECLARE @pordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50)

	DECLARE @pordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2)/*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/

	DECLARE @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId INT
	DECLARE @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)

	@pordpayImportDateTime DATETIME,

	,@pordpayDocDateTemp NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp DATETIME = NULL
	/*RZ14030129- End*/
	/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@IsTransferFreePay ,0) = 1)  /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*-------------------------------------------------*/
		Declare Crsr_Add_Payment CURSOR For
			SELECT	
				pordpayTypeId,
				pordpayDocId,
				pordpayDocTypeId,
				pordpayAmount,
				pordpayDocNumber,
				pordpayDocDate,
				pordpayDocAmount,

				,[ImportDate]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				/*RZ14000225- End*/
				,[pordpayDeActive] /*RZ14010916- Add ordpayDeActive*/
				/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/			
				,[pordpayAmountTemp]			
				/*RZ14020129- End*/
				,[pordpayAccountNumber]/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,[pordpayDocDateTemp]
				,[pordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
			FROM dbo.WSPreOrderPayments POP
			WHERE pordpayPreOrderId = @PreOrderID	
			/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			AND POP.PreOrderPaymentId NOT IN (SELECT ISNULL(OP.PreOrderPaymentId, 0)
											  FROM WSOrderPayments OP
											  WHERE ordpayOrderId IN (SELECT OrderId 
																	  FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
																	  WHERE O.PreOrderID = POP.pordpayPreOrderId) )
			AND (pordpayDocNumber NOT IN (
					SELECT ISNULL(ordpayDocNumber, 0)
					FROM WSOrderPayments
					WHERE ordpayOrderId IN (SELECT OrderId 
											FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
											WHERE O.PreOrderID = POP.pordpayPreOrderId) )

					OR ISNULL(pordpayDocNumber,'') = ''
					)
			/*RZ14010531- End*/
			AND ISNULL([pordpayDeActive],0) = 0/*RZ14020306- Add verify order after issue and not send payDeActive */		
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Payment
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Payment INTO @pordpayTypeId, @pordpayDocId, @pordpayDocTypeId,

											@pordpayDocAmount, @pordpayDescription, @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId,

		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0

				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/

				,[ordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
			)

				/*@RegisterDatePersian,*/

			INSERT INTO dbo.WSOrderPayments_Lang (

			  ,ordpayDescription		 	    			  
			)

				POPLN.pordpayDescription
			FROM dbo.WSPreOrderPayments_Lang POPLN 
			WHERE  POPLN.PreOrderPaymentId = @PreOrderPaymentId
		
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Payment INTO  @pordpayTypeId, @pordpayDocId, @pordpayDocTypeId,
											@pordpayAmount, @pordpayDocNumber, @pordpayDocDate, 					
											@pordpayDocAmount, @pordpayDescription, @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId,
											@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian, @pordpayFirstRegisteDate, @pordpayDocDateTime, @PreOrderPaymentId,

											/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
											,@pordpayDocDateTemp,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
