	DECLARE @PreOrderCommissionId INT,@CommDateTime DATETIME,@CommDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@CommCmpId INT,@CommUserId INT,@CommBaseAmountDec NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommBaseAmountDecDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommBaseAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommRate FLOAT,@CommRateDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@CommAmountCurrUnId INT,@CommAmountCurrencyRate FLOAT,@CommEffectiveDateTime DATETIME,
	@CommEffectiveDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),@CommPayTypeId INT,@CommStatusId INT,
	@CommDesc NVARCHAR(4000),@CommIsVerified BIT,@CommVerifierUserId INT,
	@CommVerifiedDateTime DATETIME,@CommVerifiedDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@CommIsPaid BIT,@CommIsPaidUserId INT,@CommIsPaidDateTime DATETIME,
	@CommIsPaidDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId INT
	/*RZ14030119- Add Type and Kind to OrderCommission*/
	,@CommTypeId INT = 1 /*User*/
	,@CommKindId INT = 1 /*Sale*/	
	/*RZ14030119- End*/
	/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@IsTransferFreeComm,0) = 1) /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*-------------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE Crsr_Add_Comm CURSOR FOR

		SELECT 
			 [PreOrderCommissionId]
			,[CommDateTime]
			,[CommDatePersian]     
			,[CommCmpId]
			,[CommUserId]
			,[CommBaseAmountDec]
			,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
			,[CommBaseAmount]
			,[CommRate]
			,[CommRateDesc]
			,[CommAmount]
			,[CommAmountCurrUnId]
			,[CommAmountCurrencyRate]
			,[CommEffectiveDateTime]
			,[CommEffectiveDatePersian]
			,[CommPayTypeId]
			,[CommStatusId]
			,[CommDesc]
			,[CommIsVerified]
			,[CommVerifierUserId]
			,[CommVerifiedDateTime]
			,[CommVerifiedDatePersian]
			,[CommIsPaid]
			,[CommIsPaidUserId]
			,[CommIsPaidDateTime]
			,[CommIsPaidDatePersian]
			,[CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId]	
		FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions]
		WHERE [CommPreOrderId] = @PreOrderID	
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		AND [PreOrderCommissionId] NOT IN (SELECT ISNULL(OC.[PreOrderCommissionId], 0)
												FROM [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions] OC
												WHERE [CommOrderId] IN (SELECT [OrderId] 
																		FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																		WHERE O.[PreOrderID] = [CommPreOrderId]))
		/*RZ14010531- End*/
		OPEN Crsr_Add_Comm
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Comm INTO @PreOrderCommissionId,@CommDateTime,@CommDatePersian     
										  ,@CommCmpId,@CommUserId,@CommBaseAmountDec,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc
										  ,@CommBaseAmount,@CommRate,@CommRateDesc,@CommAmount
										  ,@CommAmountCurrUnId,@CommAmountCurrencyRate,@CommEffectiveDateTime
										  ,@CommEffectiveDatePersian,@CommPayTypeId,@CommStatusId
										  ,@CommDesc,@CommIsVerified,@CommVerifierUserId,@CommVerifiedDateTime
										  ,@CommVerifiedDatePersian,@CommIsPaid,@CommIsPaidUserId
										  ,@CommIsPaidDateTime,@CommIsPaidDatePersian,@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId	
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @CommId INT = NULL
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions]
				([CommDateTime]
				,[CommDatePersian]
				,[CommOrderId]
				,[CommCmpId]
				,[CommUserId]
				,[CommBaseAmountDec]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommBaseAmount]
				,[CommRate]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommAmount]
				,[CommEffectiveDateTime]
				,[CommEffectiveDatePersian]
				,[CommPayTypeId]
				,[CommStatusId]
				,[CommDesc]
				/*,[CommIsVerified]
				,[CommVerifierUserId]
				,[CommVerifiedDateTime]
				,[CommVerifiedDatePersian]*/
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[UserId]
				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]			
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommIsPaid]
				,[CommIsPaidUserId]
				,[CommIsPaidDateTime]
				,[CommIsPaidDatePersian]
				,[CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId]
				,[CommAmountCurrUnId]
				,[CommAmountCurrencyRate]
				,[PreOrderCommissionId]
				/*RZ14030119- Add Type and Kind to OrderCommission*/
				,[CommTypeId]
				,[CommKindId]	
				/*RZ14030119- End*/
				)
			VALUES
				(@CommDateTime
				,@CommDatePersian
				,@OrderId
				,@CommCmpId
				,@CommUserId
				,@CommBaseAmountDec
				,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc
				,@CommBaseAmount
				,@CommRate
				,@CommRateDesc
				,@CommAmount
				,@CommEffectiveDateTime
				,@CommEffectiveDatePersian
				,@CommPayTypeId
				,@CommStatusId
				,@CommDesc
				/*,@CommIsVerified
				,@CommVerifierUserId
				,@CommVerifiedDateTime
				,@CommVerifiedDatePersian*/
				,@UserId
				,GETDATE()
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,GETDATE()
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@AppType
				,@LanguageID
				,@CommIsPaid
				,@CommIsPaidUserId
				,@CommIsPaidDateTime
				,@CommIsPaidDatePersian
				,@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId
				,@CommAmountCurrUnId
				,@CommAmountCurrencyRate
				,@PreOrderCommissionId
				/*RZ14030119- Add Type and Kind to OrderCommission*/
				,@CommTypeId
				,@CommKindId	
				/*RZ14030119- End*/
				)

			SET @CommId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions_Lang]
				([OrderCommissionId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommDesc])
			SELECT
				 @CommId
				,[LanguageID]
				,[CommBaseAmountDecDesc]
				,[CommRateDesc]
				,[CommDesc]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderCommissions_Lang]
			WHERE [PreOrderCommissionId] = @PreOrderCommissionId
				
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Comm INTO @PreOrderCommissionId,@CommDateTime,@CommDatePersian     
											   ,@CommCmpId,@CommUserId,@CommBaseAmountDec,@CommBaseAmountDecDesc
											   ,@CommBaseAmount,@CommRate,@CommRateDesc,@CommAmount
											   ,@CommAmountCurrUnId,@CommAmountCurrencyRate,@CommEffectiveDateTime
											   ,@CommEffectiveDatePersian,@CommPayTypeId,@CommStatusId
											   ,@CommDesc,@CommIsVerified,@CommVerifierUserId,@CommVerifiedDateTime
											   ,@CommVerifiedDatePersian,@CommIsPaid,@CommIsPaidUserId
											   ,@CommIsPaidDateTime,@CommIsPaidDatePersian,@CommIsPaidPayRollUserCycleId	
		/**/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Comm
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Comm	
		/*-------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	
	/*------------------- tran4-----------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrderCommissions' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 	
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Comm
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Comm
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*------------------- tran4-----------------------*/	
	/*
	------------------- tran4
	--==============================================
	--==============================================
	--==============================================
	--************/
	/*RZ990916- End*/
	/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
		@OrderId = @InsertedOrderID,
		@Month1 = @Month1,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID
	/**/
	END
	/*RZ14010531- End*/
	/*----------------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran5-----------------------*/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordTotalAmount BIGINT 
	SET @ordTotalAmount = 
	(SELECT ISNULL(ordTotalAmount, 0) FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID AND ordStatusId <> 4) 
	/**/
	DECLARE @RemainordAmount BIGINT 
	SET @RemainordAmount = 
	(SELECT ISNULL(ordTotalAmount, 0) FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID AND ordStatusId <> 4)
	-
	(SELECT ISNULL( SUM(ordpayAmount), 0) sumpayAmount
	FROM dbo.WSOrderPayments
	WHERE ordpayOrderId = @InsertedOrderID)
	/**/
	IF @RemainordAmount = 0
	BEGIN 
	UPDATE WSOrder SET ordStatusId = 3	/*����� ���*/
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID
	END
	ELSE
	IF (@RemainordAmount > 0  AND @RemainordAmount <  @ordTotalAmount)
	BEGIN
	UPDATE WSOrder SET ordStatusId = 2	/*���� ����� ����*/
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID
	END
	ELSE
	IF @RemainordAmount =  @ordTotalAmount
	BEGIN
	UPDATE WSOrder SET ordStatusId = 1	/*����� ����*/
	WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID
	END
	/**/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='UPDATE ordStatusId ' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*------------------- tran5-----------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------- OrdIndex START --------------*/
		SET @PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId = NULL

		SET @ProductId = NULL

		/*----------- Find order index setting ---------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordIssueIndexedByPrdId,0) <> 0) /*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @ProductId = @ordIssueIndexedByPrdId
			SELECT TOP(1) 
				 @ProductId = ProductID
				,@PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId = [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]
				,@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed = [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed]
				,@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId = [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId]
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
			WHERE ProductID = @ProductId
			AND ISNULL([PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed],0) = 1
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ProductId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @IndexedOrdId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdDailyCounter BIGINT = NULL,
				@IndexedOrdNumber BIGINT = NULL			
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				SET @IndexedOrdDailyCounter = (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter](@PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId, @OrdDateTime))
				SET @IndexedOrdNumber =  (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderNumber](@FPId, @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId))
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
				SET @ordIsVerified = NULL
				SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDate = NULL
				SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = NULL
				SET @ordVerifierId  = NULL
				SET @VerifyAfterIssue = NULL
				/**/
				SET @VerifyAfterIssue = (SELECT TOP(1) [VerifyAfterIssue] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] WHERE OrderOprId = @PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId)
				IF (ISNULL(@VerifyAfterIssue,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIsVerified = 1
					SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDate = GETDATE()
					SET @ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@ordVerifiedRegisterDate AS DATE)))

						DECLARE @IndexedOrderItemId INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,

						@IndexedOrdIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,

						@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,

						/**/

						SET @IndexedOrdIOrderView = (@IndexedOrdItemCount + 1) + (@IndexedOrdItemCount * 4 ) - 1
						/*-------*/
						SET @PrdValidityDay = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTime = NULL
						SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = NULL

						/**/
						SELECT  
							 @PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
							,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]

							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo = [PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate]/*RZ14020621- Change fields name*/
						FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]

						AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

						IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
						BEGIN
						/**/

						/**/

							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate))
						/**/

							,@IndexedOrdIPrdNickname						
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate

						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrderItemId,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
								([OrderItemID]

								,[ordIPrdNickname])
							SELECT 
								 @IndexedOrderItemId
								,[LanguageID]
								,[PrdNickname]
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
							WHERE ProductID = @IndexedOrdIPrdId
						/**/
						ENd
						/*------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
							@OrderId = @IndexedOrdId,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
							@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID
						/**/
						END
						/*------------------------------------*/	
					/**/
					END
					/*------------------------------------*/		
				/*------------------- tran6 --------------------*/
				IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN 
					SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrder For PrdIndexed' 
					SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 				
				ROLLBACK TRAN 
				END		
				ELSE 
				COMMIT TRAN 		
				/*----------------------------------------*/
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END				
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------- OrdIndex END ----------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*-------- Update prjRemindDate START ----------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		SET @ProductId = NULL
		SET @PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDate = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDateTime = NULL
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*------- Find product validty for update prjRemindDate ---------*/
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			 @ProductId = ProductID
			,@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd = [PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd]			
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
		WHERE ProductID IN(SELECT TOP(1) pordIPrdId			
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
							AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [
